Conclusions: The proposed system enhances the transparency of traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning and the interpretability of treatment plans through knowledge graph-driven visualization and multimodal interaction, offering a practical solution for trustworthy artificial intelligence-assisted traditional Chinese medicine applications.

</details>


### [79] [Workflow-to-Skill: Skill Creation via Routing-Workflow-Semantics-Attachments Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06893)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xinyuan Han,Xudong Jiang,Run Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RWSA中间表示和W2S框架，从异构交互迹中自动构建高质量技能，相比摘要基线提升了10.5%的行为回放一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量技能的手写成本高昂，而从异构交互迹（演示、轨迹、工具痕迹、执行日志）中自动构建技能并非简单的摘要任务，因为迹存在碎片化、冗余且可能缺失罕见但安全关键的行为。

Method: 提出RWSA（面向工作流的中间表示），将技能分解为工作流结构、执行语义和运行时附件；在此基础上提出W2S框架，包含迹分段、局部技能草稿诱导、共享结构对齐、分支调和以及冗余压缩，同时保留证据和置信度标注。

Result: 在70个技能上的实验表明，W2S相比基于摘要和提示的基线，将行为回放一致性提升了10.5%。

Conclusion: 应将迹视为可执行的运行时规约而非可压缩的文本，才能实现高质量的技能自动构建。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on Skills to encode procedural knowledge, yet high-quality Skills remain costly to hand-write. This paper studies automatic Skill construction from heterogeneous interaction evidence, including demonstrations, agent trajectories, tool traces, and execution logs. We argue that trace-to-skill construction is not simple summarization tasks, because traces are fragmented, redundant, and may miss rare but safety-critical behaviors. To address this, we introduce RWSA, a workflow-oriented intermediate representation that decomposes Skills into Workflow structure, execution Semantics, and runtime Attachments, capturing task decomposition, control flow, verification, safety, rollback, and state management. Building on RWSA, we propose W2S, a framework that segments traces, induces local Skill drafts, aligns shared structures, reconciles branches, and compresses redundancy while preserving evidence and confidence annotations. Experiments on 70 Skills show that W2S improves behavioral replay consistency by 10.5% over summarization- and prompting-based baselines, highlighting the need to treat traces as executable runtime specifications rather than compressible text.

</details>


### [80] [Declarative Skills for AI Agents in Knowledge-Grounded Tool-Use Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06923)
*M. Danish Lim,I. Danial Bin Sharudin,Wen Han Chen,Cedric Lim,Laura Wynter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于非结构化知识库的客服工作流中，工具型AI智能体的编排机制，比较了声明式智能体（通过自然语言技能文件实现）、命令式智能体（基于程序化状态机）和无脚手架基线智能体三种范式，发现检索质量是主要瓶颈，在高检索质量下声明式技能可提升程序性任务准确率并减少编排错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在真实客服工作流中，哪种智能体编排范式（声明式vs命令式vs无脚手架基线）更有效，并理解检索质量对智能体性能的影响。

Method: 将三种智能体形式化为分散式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP）中的策略类，分析其信息论与结构特性，并在5种语言模型和两种检索机制下进行实证比较。

Result: 检索质量是主导瓶颈；低质量检索下所有智能体性能严重下降；高质量检索下声明式技能可提升程序性任务准确率并减少编排错误，命令式状态机的脆弱性无法可靠提升任务成功率和合规性。

Conclusion: 声明式智能体（自然语言技能文件）是一种有效的编排范式，但检索质量是更关键的瓶颈因素。

Abstract: We study orchestration mechanisms for tool-using AI agents in realistic customer-service workflows over an unstructured knowledge base. We argue that declarative agents -- AI agents equipped with natural-language skill files appended to the system prompt -- are an effective orchestration paradigm. Concretely, we compare (i) a DeclarativeAgent that reads three domain-specific skill files at inference time and decides its own control flow, (ii) an ImperativeAgent based on a programmatic state machine with explicit phases, and (iii) an unscaffolded baseline agent modeled after the $τ$-Knowledge benchmark agent. Our ImperativeAgent is motivated by externalised-control inference as in Recursive Language Models and graph-based orchestration frameworks. We formalise the three agents as policy classes within a decentralised partially-observable Markov decision process and analyse their information-theoretic and structural properties; we then test the predicted differences empirically on five language models and two retrieval regimes. Our results show that retrieval quality is a dominant bottleneck for AI agents: when evidence is incomplete or skewed, all agents degrade substantially, and skill files cannot recover lost performance. Under high-quality retrieval, however, declarative skills consistently improve accuracy on procedural tasks and reduce orchestration errors, while the imperative state machine's brittleness does not reliably improve task success or compliance.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum-Inspired Trace-Augmented Evidence Selection for Reasoning over Structured Hypothesis Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06941)
*Laura Wynter,Nirvik Sahoo,Paul Griffin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EP-HUBO方法，将CoT推理片段选择视为组合优化问题，通过HUBO优化聚合证据，在证据密集型法律推理任务上优于多数投票，尤其适合低污染领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在法律等证据密集型领域表现脆弱，多数投票聚合CoT轨迹时忽视证据质量，导致证据充分但非主流的正确假设被噪声多数压制。

Method: 用小模型生成多条CoT轨迹，将推理片段解析为每假设的证据池；构建高阶无约束二元优化（HUBO）问题，以相关性、特异性、区分性为权重求解；每个问题仅由前沿模型做一次最终裁决。

Result: 在两个证据密集型法律基准上，EP-HUBO在经典模拟退火和狄拉克-3光子熵量子机上都取得优于多数投票的表现，尤其在低污染领域优势明显。

Conclusion: HUBO式优化提供了一种原则性的推理片段聚合方式，能保留少数但正确的假设，是证据密集型低污染领域的有力工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now solve a wide range of expert-level exams at or above human level, yet remain brittle on specialised, evidence-intensive domains such as law. On these tasks, errors arise not only from gaps in world knowledge but also from subtle distinctions between pieces of evidence and inconsistent use of supporting evidence. The most common aggregator over sampled chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, majority vote, returns the most popular answer regardless of whether its evidence is actually strongest. We propose to treat the selection of CoT reasoning fragments into a set of evidence as an explicit combinatorial optimisation problem, allowing well-supported but minority hypotheses to override noisy majorities, and to evaluate the approach on legal-reasoning benchmarks that are particularly sensitive to evidence quality. We introduce EP-HUBO (Evidence Pool Higher-Order Binary Optimisation), which generates multiple CoT traces with a small local model, parses fragments into per-hypothesis evidence pools, solves a higher-order unconstrained binary optimisation per pool with quality-derived weights (relevance, specificity, distinctiveness), and delegates a single adjudication call per question to a frontier model. We evaluate EP-HUBO on two evidence-intensive legal benchmarks using both simulated annealing on classical hardware and the Dirac-3 photonic entropy-quantum machine from Quantum Computing Inc. HUBO-style optimisation gives a principled way to aggregate reasoning fragments while preserving minority-but-correct hypotheses, and is most valuable in low-contamination domains where frontier models have not already absorbed the benchmark material.

</details>


### [82] [Accounting for Context: Shaping Moral Credences for Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06972)
*Jazon Szabo,Sanjay Modgil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在道德不确定性下聚合不同道德理论评估时，需要考虑情境因素（如结果主义对行动后果的认知能力），否则会违反弱帕累托原则，这本质上是辛普森悖论的一种变体，揭示了忽略情境因素的聚合机制的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于道德不确定性的工作提出了在不同道德理论间公平、民主地聚合行为评估的机制，但忽略了情境因素（如结果主义假设能准确预测行动后果，这在现实中通常不成立）对聚合结果的影响。

Method: 形式化建模智能体在道德不确定性下的决策过程，同时纳入情境因素对道德评估的影响，并通过理论分析检验聚合机制的性质。

Result: 证明了在考虑情境因素后，一个看似常识性的性质——弱帕累托原则——会被违反；进一步指出这一现象是辛普森悖论的一种变体。

Conclusion: 揭示了忽略情境因素的道德评估聚合机制存在根本性局限，为设计更合理的多道德视角对齐方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Ensuring that agent behaviours are aligned with human moral values inevitably raises the problem of how to account for the plurality of moral perspectives that societies -- and even individuals -- typically adopt. Work on moral uncertainty proposes mechanisms to fairly and democratically aggregate evaluations of actions across different moral theories. However, this paper argues that one needs to account for contextual factors when aggregating moral evaluations. For example, consequentialist perspectives assume an ability to accurately determine how an agent's actions change the world; an assumption that often does not hold in real world settings. We, therefore, formalise agent decision making under moral uncertainty, while also accounting for these kinds of contextual factors. We thereby show that a seemingly commonsensical property -- the weak Pareto principle -- is violated. We argue that this apparent problem is, in fact, a variation of Simpson's paradox, and hence reveals the limitations of aggregation mechanisms that ignore the impact of contextual factors.

</details>


### [83] [Exploring Agentic Tool-Calling Decisions via Uncertainty-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06976)
*Yijin Zhou,Linqian Zeng,Xiaoya Lu,Wenyuan Xie,Dongrui Liu,Junchi Yan,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRUST方法，通过将不确定性量化作为排斥力引入奖励设计，并标注关键轮次进行统一后训练，以改善LLM智能体在工具使用中的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖推理时修正或基于决策结果的粗粒度奖励信号，忽略了智能体决策的不确定性特征；决策导向的强化学习会削弱正确与错误行动之间的不确定性分离，导致过度自信的错误和较弱的探索信号。

Method: 提出TRUST框架：1) 将不确定性量化作为排斥力纳入奖励设计，以维持正确与错误行动之间的不确定性分离；2) 标注轻量级关键轮次标签，用于多轮轨迹的统一后训练。

Result: 在多个工具使用基准测试中，TRUST一致提升了决策质量和智能体性能，并在优化过程中保持了更可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化可以有效改善LLM智能体的工具使用决策，TRUST方法通过维持不确定性分离和关键轮次标注实现更优的决策优化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents often make suboptimal tool-use decisions, including unsupported tool invocation and hallucinated direct responses, which may accumulate errors throughout multi-step interactions. Existing approaches mainly improve these behaviors through inference-time correction or coarse-grained reward signals based on decision outcomes and structured checklists, leaving the uncertainty characteristics of agent decisions underexplored. We observe that decision-oriented reinforcement learning tends to weaken the uncertainty separation between correct and incorrect actions, resulting in overconfident mistakes and weaker exploration signals. Therefore, we propose TRUST, which incorporates uncertainty quantification into reward design as a repulsive force for maintaining uncertainty separation, and labels lightweight key-turn annotations for unified post-training of multi-turn trajectories. Experimental results across diverse tool-use benchmarks show that TRUST consistently enhances both decision quality and agent performance while maintaining more reliable uncertainty estimates during optimization.

</details>


### [84] [Teaching the Way, Not the Answer: Privileged Tutoring Distillation for Multimodal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07000)
*Shizhe Xiang,Ke An,Wenlong Yu,Yue Liu,Jian Luan,Pei Fu,Qilong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLVR在复杂多模态推理中奖励稀疏、探索效率低的问题，提出PTD-PO框架，通过特权教学蒸馏策略，在不暴露答案的情况下提供密集的token级监督，并引入Top-K JS散度稳定分布偏移，在2B-8B LVLM上超越RLVR和蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法中可验证奖励稀疏，无法为失败轨迹提供token级监督，导致多模态推理探索效率低；外部教师蒸馏计算开销大，答案条件微调会暴露答案信息并引发捷径行为。

Method: PTD-PO框架从空间注意力引导和中间文本推理步骤构建结构化特权提示，通过上下文学习生成逐步token分布监督；学生策略在原始无答案上下文中优化，将失败rollout与提示增强参考模型在token分布层面对齐；引入Top-K JS散度目标聚焦信息性token概率对齐并降低内存开销。

Result: 在2B到8B参数的LVLM上，PTD-PO持续优于RLVR和蒸馏基线，缓解了熵坍塌，提升了复杂多模态推理性能。

Conclusion: PTD-PO通过特权教学蒸馏策略，在不暴露答案的前提下为RLVR提供密集监督信号，有效解决了稀疏奖励下的探索困难问题，是一种提升多模态推理能力的有效框架。

Abstract: Recent post-training methods, particularly Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), have significantly enhanced the reasoning ability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). However, the sparse nature of verifiable rewards provides little token-level supervision for failed rollouts, often leading to inefficient exploration in complex multimodal reasoning tasks. Although policy distillation can offer dense guidance, external teacher based methods introduce substantial computational overhead, while answer conditioned tuning methods may expose answer-level information and induce shortcut-like generation behavior. To address these limitations, we propose PTD-PO, a Privileged Tutoring Distillation Policy Optimization framework for RLVR that provides dense guidance without exposing the answer to the student policy. Specifically, PTD-PO constructs structured privileged hints from spatial attention guidance and intermediate textual reasoning steps, and uses them through in-context learning to produce step-wise token-distribution supervision. The student is still optimized under the original answer-free context, and its failed rollouts are aligned with the hint-augmented reference model at the token-distribution level. To further stabilize distillation under the distribution shift between guided and unguided contexts, we introduce a Top-K Jensen-Shannon divergence objective that focuses alignment on informative token probabilities while reducing memory overhead. Experiments on LVLMs ranging from 2B to 8B parameters show that PTD-PO consistently outperforms RLVR and distillation baselines, mitigates entropy collapse, and improves complex multimodal reasoning performance.

</details>


### [85] [The Sim-to-Real Gap of Foundation Model Agents: A Unified MDP Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07017)
*Xiaoou Liu,Tiejin Chen,Weibo Li,Xiyang Hu,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将基础模型智能体的鲁棒性问题形式化为经典的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）问题，围绕MDP的四个要素（观察、动作、转移、奖励）构建统一框架，倡导采用领域随机化等成熟解决方案，并旨在推动建立标准化压力测试基准以实现更可信赖的现实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体在现实世界部署中面临仿真到现实的鸿沟（sim-to-real gap），但该领域却将智能体鲁棒性视为全新现象，未能借鉴机器人学和经典控制中成熟的解决框架。作者希望弥合这一认知差距。

Method: 将基础模型智能体的评估与训练差距形式化为经典的sim-to-real问题，围绕MDP的四个核心要素（Observation, Action, Transition, Reward）进行结构化解构，并倡导采用领域随机化（domain randomization）等已有成熟方案。

Result: 提供了具体实例（如多语言工具调用），展示了观察空间差距如何导致语义正确但操作无效的动作；提出了统一词汇表和标准化压力测试基准的研究议程。

Conclusion: 旨在推动范式转变，通过建立统一框架和标准化基准，催生新一代高可信度的基础模型智能体，实现可靠的现实世界应用。

Abstract: Foundation model agents are increasingly deployed for real-world decision-making, but suffer from the sim-to-real gap. While robotics and classical control have mature frameworks to address this gap, the foundation model community is treating agent robustness as an entirely novel phenomenon. Our paper proposes formalizing the foundation model agent evaluation and training gap as a classical sim-to-real problem structured entirely around the four elements of a Markov Decision Process, including Observation, Action, Transition, and Reward. In this paper, we set a comprehensive research agenda that translates classical discrepancies into the foundation model domain and advocates for adopting established solutions like domain randomization. We provide concrete examples, such as a multilingual tool calling to demonstrate how severe observation space gaps lead to operationally invalid actions despite correct semantic intent. Ultimately, this agenda aims to drive a paradigm shift, yielding a unified vocabulary and standardized stress test benchmarks to foster a new generation of highly trustworthy agents for reliable real-world applications.

</details>


### [86] [StainFlow: Entity-Stain Tracking and Evidence Linking for Process Rewards in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07027)
*Haojie Hao,Longkun Hao,Yihang Lou,Yan Bai,Zhenyang Li,Zhichao Yang,Dongshuo Huang,Hongyu Lin,Lanqing Hong,Jiakai Wang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StainFlow，一种基于实体污点追踪机制的过程奖励模型（PRM），用于强化学习训练GUI智能体。它通过全局实体污点追踪模块客观划分任务阶段，以及局部污点证据链接模块动态构建高密度证据窗口验证关键节点，解决了现有PRM在全局里程碑分解主观单一和局部判断窗口视野受限的问题。实验表明该方法在online RL成功率和轨迹完成判断准确率上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长视野、随机的数字环境中，轨迹级成功反馈过于稀疏，无法为中间探索步骤提供可靠的信用分配。现有PRM方法存在两个层面的局限：全局里程碑分解主观且单一，难以适应GUI任务中多条有效执行路径；固定局部判断窗口可能遗漏长程关键证据或用无关帧稀释决策信号。

Method: 受网络流分析中污点追踪机制启发，提出StainFlow，包含两个核心模块：(1) 全局实体污点追踪模块——提取可视化任务实体，追踪其污点浓度和状态沿轨迹的演变，根据实体证据流的变化客观划分任务阶段；(2) 局部污点证据链接模块——以每个候选关键节点的触发实体为中心，基于污点浓度和状态变化检索相关步骤，动态构建高密度证据窗口用于验证真正关键节点。

Result: 在AndroidWorld和OGRBench基准上的实验表明，StainFlow在online RL成功率上相对提升3.2%，在轨迹完成判断准确率上相对提升1.8%。

Conclusion: StainFlow通过实体级污点追踪机制，有效解决了现有PRM在全局分解和局部验证上的局限性，为GUI智能体的强化学习训练提供了更可靠的过程奖励信号，在多个基准上取得了性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become a promising approach for improving GUI Agents in long-horizon, stochastic digital environments, but trajectory-level success feedback is too sparse to provide reliable credit assignment for intermediate exploration steps. To mitigate this issue, recent studies introduce Process Reward Models (PRMs), which provide finer-grained training feedback through global milestone verification or local step-level evaluation. However, these methods still suffer from two level-specific limitations: global milestone decomposition is subjective and singular, making it difficult to accommodate the multiple valid execution paths in real GUI tasks, while fixed local judging windows may miss long-range key evidence or dilute the decision signal with irrelevant frames. Inspired by stain-tracing mechanisms in network flow analysis, we propose StainFlow, an entity-stain-flow process reward model for GUI Agents. To reduce the subjectivity of global partitioning, we introduce the Global Entity Stain Tracking module, which extracts visually verifiable task entities and tracks how their stain concentrations and states evolve along the trajectory, allowing task phases to be objectively separated by changes in the entity evidence flow. To improve the accuracy of local verification, we introduce the Local Stain Evidence Linking module. Centered on the triggering entities of each candidate key node, it retrieves relevant steps based on their stain concentrations and state changes, and dynamically constructs high-density evidence windows for verifying true key nodes. Extensive experiments on AndroidWorld and OGRBench show that StainFlow relatively improves online RL success by 3.2% and trajectory completion judgment accuracy by 1.8%.

</details>


### [87] [Hierarchical Semantic-Constrained Heterogeneous Graph for Audio-Visual Event Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07033)
*Zhe Yang,Ruyi Zhang,Hongtao Chen,Wenrui Li,Hengyu Man,Wangmeng Zuo,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层语义约束异构图（HSCHG）框架，通过欧几里得空间中的异构分层图建模多模态时序信息，结合双阈值滤波门控融合策略和双曲空间中的分层蕴含正则化损失，解决了开放词汇视听事件定位中跨模态一致性和跨层级语义一致性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇视听事件定位方法主要基于欧几里得空间的联合视听表征学习，面临两大挑战：一是缺乏对不可见类别的监督信号，难以在多个时间尺度上维持视听一致性；二是缺乏片段级与视频级语义之间的分层约束，无法建立跨层级的语义一致性。

Method: 1）构建欧几里得空间中的异构分层图，包含音频/视觉片段节点及对应的视频级节点；2）使用多方向时序边捕获模态内完整时序信息；3）采用双阈值滤波门控融合策略，仅在对齐置信度高时引入跨模态信息；4）引入片段级与视频级表征间的双向语义约束；5）将多层级视听表征与文本原型统一映射到双曲空间，使用分层蕴含正则化损失建模视频与片段间的层级关系。

Result: 在OV-AVEL基准测试上，该方法优于现有方法；消融实验进一步验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过欧几里得空间与双曲空间的协同建模，结合分层语义约束，有效解决了开放词汇场景下跨模态和跨层级的不一致问题，为AVEL任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary audio-visual event localization (OV-AVEL) jointly models audio-visual cues to recognize and temporally localize events, including categories unseen during training. Existing methods primarily learn joint audio-visual representations in Euclidean space, but still face two significant challenges. First, the lack of supervision signals for unseen categories makes it difficult to maintain audio-visual consistency across multiple temporal scales. Second, the lack of hierarchical constraints between segment- and video-level semantics prevents the model from establishing semantic consistency across different levels. To address these challenges, we propose a hierarchical semantic constrained heterogeneous graph (HSCHG) for audio-visual event localization framework. We first construct a heterogeneous hierarchical graph in Euclidean space, which includes audio and visual segment nodes and their corresponding video-level nodes. We use multi-directional temporal edges to capture complete temporal information within each modality. Simultaneously, we employ a dual-threshold filtering gated fusion strategy, introducing cross-modal information only when the alignment confidence is high. Furthermore, we introduce bidirectional semantic constraints between segment- and video-level representations to achieve semantic consistency across different levels. Based on this, we map the multi-level audio-visual representations and text prototypes uniformly into hyperbolic space. We use a hierarchical entailment regularization loss to characterize the hierarchical relationships between videos and segments. Extensive experimental results show that our method outperforms existing methods on the OV-AVEL benchmark. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [88] [Front-to-Attractors: Modifying the Front-to-Front Heuristic in Bidirectional Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07047)
*Alvin Zou,Muhammad Suhail Saleem,Maxim Likhachev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的双向搜索启发式类别——前向-吸引子（Front-to-Attractors, F2A），通过维护少量动态吸引子替代完整对向前沿，在保持F2F启发式信息性的同时大幅降低计算开销，实验显示相较F2F减少了最多11.2倍的配对评估次数，相较F2E平均减少了4.8倍的节点扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向搜索中，前向-前端（F2F）启发式虽信息丰富、节点扩展少，但依赖大量配对评估导致计算开销巨大；而前向-末端（F2E）启发式计算高效但信息性不足。为此，本文旨在设计一种兼具F2F信息性与F2E高效性的启发式类别。

Method: 提出前向-吸引子（F2A）启发式类别：不评估当前节点s到对向前沿所有状态的距离，而是估计s到对向搜索方向中一个动态维护的小型吸引子集合的距离，以吸引子作为完整前沿的替代，实现低计算开销下的丰富启发式引导，同时保留F2F的最优性保证。

Result: 在多个领域上的实验表明，F2A相较F2F最多减少了11.2倍的配对评估次数，相较F2E平均减少了4.8倍的节点扩展。

Conclusion: F2A在保持最优性保证的前提下，有效平衡了启发式的信息性与计算效率，为双向搜索提供了一种实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Heuristics play a central role in the performance of bidirectional search algorithms, which commonly rely on two main classes. Front-to-end (F2E) heuristics estimate the distance from a state s to the target of the search (the goal for forward search or the start for backward search). In contrast, front-to-front (F2F) heuristics estimate the distance from s to the opposite search frontier using a pairwise function h(s, s'), where s' ranges over frontier states. Although F2F heuristics are typically more informative and therefore reduce the number of node expansions, their reliance on extensive pairwise evaluations incurs substantial computational overhead. To address this limitation, we introduce a new heuristic class, front-to-attractors (F2A), that preserves much of the informativeness of F2F while dramatically reducing its computational cost. Rather than evaluating distances to all states on the opposite frontier, F2A estimates the distance from s to a small, dynamically maintained set of attractors in the opposite search direction. These attractors serve as a surrogate for the full frontier, enabling rich heuristic guidance at a fraction of the computational expense while maintaining the optimality guarantees offered by F2F. We evaluate F2A across multiple domains and show that it reduces the number of pairwise evaluations by up to 11.2x compared to F2F, while achieving 4.8x fewer node expansions than F2E on average.

</details>


### [89] [DyCon: Dynamic Reasoning Control via Evolving Difficulty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07108)
*Tengyao Tu,Yulin Li,Hui-Ling Zhen,Libo Qin,Zhoujun Wei,Jinghua Piao,Zhuotao Tian,Yong Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DyCon通过利用大推理模型的步骤级嵌入动态建模任务难度，在无需训练的情况下控制推理深度，减少“过度思考”导致的冗余步骤，显著提升推理效率且不损失准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型存在“过度思考”问题（冗余推理导致效率低下），已有缓解方法依赖静态难度估计或需任务特定训练，无法适应推理过程中动态变化的复杂性。

Method: DyCon：一种无需训练的框架，利用步骤级潜在表示显式建模推理过程中动态演变的任务难度，从而动态控制推理深度。

Result: 在4B到32B的四个模型、十二个基准（数学推理、通用问答、编程任务）上，DyCon显著提升推理效率，减少冗余步骤，且不牺牲准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 问题难度在推理过程中动态演变，且线性编码于LRM的步骤级嵌入中；DyCon通过动态控制推理深度有效缓解过度思考问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) demonstrate remarkable performance improvements by iteratively reflecting, exploring, and executing complex tasks, yet suffer from inefficiencies due to redundant reasoning, known as "overthinking". Existing methods to mitigate this issue either rely on static difficulty estimates or require task-specific training, and thus fail to adapt to the dynamic complexity during reasoning. In this work, we empirically show that the problem difficulty evolves dynamically throughout the reasoning process and is linearly encoded in the LRM's step-level embeddings. Building on this insight, we propose DyCon, a training-free framework that leverages latent step-level representations to explicitly model the evolving task difficulty, enabling the dynamic control of reasoning depth to mitigate the overthinking issue. Extensive experiments conducted on four models ranging from 4B to 32B, and across twelve benchmarks in math reasoning, general question answering, and coding tasks demonstrate that DyCon significantly enhances reasoning efficiency by reducing redundant steps without sacrificing accuracy or generalization. Project page and code are available at https://github.com/yu-lin-li/DyCon.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Post-hoc Explanation: Toward Glassbox AI via Probabilistic Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07113)
*Manuele Leonelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Glassbox框架，使用贝叶斯网络作为生成模型的透明事前中介层，实现可审计的推理轨迹、不确定性量化和可质疑输出，从而将AI系统从事后解释转向事前概率中介，使其具备根本性的可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险机构场景中日益成为基础设施，但其不透明性在制度上和法律上不可持续；现有事后解释方法提供的解释不稳定、不可质疑，且与模型实际推理过程无形式化关联。问题的根源不在于缺乏解释，而在于缺乏结构化的推理过程本身。

Method: 提出Glassbox框架，以贝叶斯网络作为生成模型的事前中介层：在推理前编码领域知识、因果假设和概率依赖关系，实现透明、可审计的推理轨迹、不确定性量化及可质疑输出。

Result: 在理论层面刻画了该框架的架构，并通过福利资格审核场景进行具体化阐述；识别出实现规模化部署需解决的四大基础挑战：语义对齐、动态模型构建、概率基础与人类治理。

Conclusion: 从事后解释转向事前概率中介，为构建既强大又可根本性问责的AI系统提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Large language models are rapidly becoming infrastructural components in high-stakes institutional settings, including public administration, legal reasoning, and healthcare, where opacity is not merely inconvenient but institutionally and legally untenable. Existing approaches to explainability are predominantly post-hoc, offering unstable, non-contestable accounts that have no formal relationship to the reasoning process that produced the output. We argue that the problem is not the absence of explanation but the absence of structured reasoning in the first place. This paper makes the case for a fundamentally different architecture, which we call the Glassbox Framework, in which Bayesian networks serve as transparent, ante-hoc mediation layers for generative models. Bayesian networks encode domain knowledge, causal assumptions, and probabilistic dependencies before inference occurs, enabling auditable reasoning traces, uncertainty quantification, and contestable outputs. We characterise the architecture of this framework and ground it in a benefit eligibility scenario, identifying the foundational challenges spanning semantic alignment, dynamic model construction, probabilistic grounding, and human governance that must be solved to realise it at scale. By shifting from post-hoc explanation to ante-hoc probabilistic mediation, this work outlines a principled path toward AI systems that are not only powerful but fundamentally accountable.

</details>


### [91] [Think Fast: Estimating No-CoT Task-Completion Time Horizons of Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07157)
*Dewi Gould,Francis Rhys Ward,Anders Cairns Woodruff,Rauno Arike,Josh Hills,Alex Serrano,Ida Caspary,Jason Ross Brown,Jo J. Jiao,Patrick Leask,Twm Stone,Ram Potham,Ionut Gabriel Stan,Harry Mayne,Simeon Hellsten,Shubhorup Biswas,Ariana Azarbal,William L. Anderson,Elle Najt,Ryan Greenblatt,Julian Stastny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了前沿AI模型在没有思维链（CoT）推理情况下的能力，发现无CoT的50%任务完成时间阈限在过去六年中大约每年翻倍，GPT-5.5已超过3分钟，预测到2028年可能超过7分钟，2030年超过25分钟，这对依赖监控CoT的AI安全方法构成挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多前沿AI安全措施依赖监控模型的思维链推理过程。如果模型能够在不产生显式推理token的情况下进行足够复杂的内部推理，这些监督方法将失效。因此需要量化评估模型在不使用CoT时的推理能力。

Method: 在涵盖数学、编程、谜题、因果推理、心理理论和策略推理等43个基准测试的30,000多个问题上，测量前沿模型无CoT的推理能力。使用两个指标：50%任务完成时间阈限（人类完成任务所需时间）和50%推理token阈限（o3-mini完成任务所需最小推理token数）。

Result: 前沿模型的无CoT 50%时间阈限在过去六年中大约每年翻倍，GPT-5.5的时间阈限超过3分钟，推理token阈限超过1,500个。中位数预测显示，到2028年无CoT时间阈限可能超过7分钟，到2030年超过25分钟，但预测存在较大不确定性。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的无CoT推理能力正在快速增长，可能削弱依赖CoT监控的安全措施。建议前沿AI开发者明确追踪这一指标。

Abstract: Many efforts to ensure frontier AI models are safe rely on monitoring their chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. If models become able to perform sufficiently complex reasoning internally, without explicit thinking tokens, this would undermine such oversight. We measure how well frontier models reason without CoT across a suite of over 30,000 questions spanning 43 benchmarks in domains including math, coding, puzzles, causality, theory-of-mind, and strategic reasoning. To compare models against humans, we estimate the $50\%$-task-completion time horizon (TH): the human time required for tasks a model completes with $50\%$ success rate. We complement this with a $50\%$ reasoning token horizon: the minimum number of o3-mini reasoning tokens needed for tasks a model solves with $50\%$ success rate. We find that the no-CoT $50\%$ TH of frontier models has been doubling roughly every year over the past six years, with GPT-5.5's TH reaching over 3 minutes and reasoning token horizon exceeding 1,500 tokens. Our median estimates predict that frontier no-CoT THs could exceed 7 minutes by 2028, and 25 minutes by 2030, though these projections carry substantial uncertainty. We recommend frontier developers track this explicitly.

</details>


### [92] [TOPSIS-RAD: Ranking According to Desires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07253)
*Leonardo Fernandes Costa,Helder Gomes Costa,Diogo Lima,Brunno Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TOPSIS-RAD方法，通过引入决策者定义的两个参考水平阵列（VPL和DPL）来改进传统TOPSIS方法，解决其排名易受异常值影响和排名逆转等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TOPSIS方法从观测备选方案集推导正理想解（PIS）和负理想解（NIS），导致排名易与决策者需求不一致、对异常值敏感且出现排名逆转。

Method: TOPSIS-RAD通过引入两组决策者定义的参考水平：（1）否决绩效水平（VPL）在标准化前排除不可行方案；（2）期望绩效水平（DPL）将绩效上限设定在决策者期望水平，从而锚定PIS于明确期望而非数据集极端值。

Result: 通过三个玩具示例证明：VPL通过移除不可行方案重塑标准化边界；固定DPL边界通过限制远高于期望水平的绩效影响来稳定排名。

Conclusion: 该方法在保留TOPSIS距离结构的同时，将排名建立在稳定、决策者指定的边界上，并讨论了局限性和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Traditional TOPSIS derives its reference points -- the Positive Ideal Solution ($PIS$) and Negative Ideal Solution ($NIS$) -- from the observed alternative set, making rankings susceptible to misalignment with decision-maker (DM) requirements, sensitivity to outlier performances, and rank reversal. This paper proposes TOPSIS-RAD, which addresses these issues by incorporating two arrays of DM-defined reference levels. Vetoed Performance Levels ($VPL$) exclude non-viable alternatives before normalisation, preventing them from distorting the ranking frontiers. Desired Performance Levels ($DPL$) cap performances at the DM's desired level before normalisation, anchoring the $PIS$ in explicit aspirations rather than dataset extremes. Three toy examples demonstrate each mechanism: $VPL$ reshapes normalisation boundaries by removing a non-viable alternative; fixed $DPL$ frontiers stabilise rankings by limiting the influence of performances well above the desired level. The method preserves the familiar distance-based structure of TOPSIS while grounding the ranking in stable, DM-specified boundaries. Limitations and future research directions are also discussed.

</details>


### [93] [DuMate-DeepResearch: An Auditable Multi-Agent System with Recursive Search and Rubric-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07299)
*Lingyong Yan,Can Xu,Yukun Zhao,Wenxuan Li,Qingyang Chen,Jiulong Wu,Wenli Song,Xiangnan Li,Weixian Shi,Yiqun Chen,Xuchen Ma,Yuchen Li,Jiashu Zhao,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuMate-DeepResearch 是一个基于多智能体的深度研究框架，通过解耦智能体核心与工具生态、引入图式动态规划、递归双层执行和基于规则的测试时优化机制，在两项深度研究基准上取得了最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统面临四个相互关联的局限：长程规划中任务范围不明确、单智能体任务分解与调度瓶颈、长文本合成中的幻觉风险、以及过程可审计性不足。本研究旨在解决这些问题，构建更可靠的深度研究系统。

Method: 提出多智能体DR框架DuMate-DeepResearch，核心包括：(1) 解耦Agent Core（任务理解、规划与调度）与可扩展工具生态，实现中间决策与工具调用的显式可追溯；(2) 图式动态规划策略，从粗到细扩展研究路线图，通过反思、重规划、回溯和并行分支持续修正；(3) 递归双层执行设计，将复杂搜索子任务委托给内部Search Agent独立规划，隔离噪声检索；(4) 基于规则的测试时优化机制，动态生成任务特定质量标准，作为实时推理脚手架用于证据驱动的合成和自适应停止。

Result: 在DeepResearch Bench上取得最佳综合得分58.03%，在DeepResearch Bench II上取得最佳综合得分61.95%，同时在信息召回和分析维度排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体架构和动态规划机制，DuMate-DeepResearch有效缓解了当前深度研究系统的关键瓶颈，在基准测试中达到最先进水平，验证了其方法的有效性。

Abstract: Deep Research (DR) has emerged as a new agentic paradigm to tackle complex, open-ended research tasks, demanding systems that can iteratively frame problems, acquire evidence, verify sources, and synthesize long-form reports. In practice, however, current DR systems are constrained by four interrelated limitations: long-horizon planning over an underspecified scope, the bottleneck of decomposing and scheduling such tasks within a single agent, hallucination risk in long-form synthesis, and limited process auditability. This technical report presents DuMate-DeepResearch, a multi-agent DR framework built on the Qianfan Agent Foundry. The framework decouples the Agent Core, which handles task understanding, planning, and scheduling, from an extensible Tool Ecosystem for retrieval, evidence acquisition, and report rendering, making every intermediate decision and tool invocation explicitly traceable. Building on this infrastructure, DuMate-DeepResearch further introduces three mechanisms: (i) a graph-based dynamic planning strategy expands the research roadmap coarse-to-fine and continuously revises it through reflection, re-planning, backtracking, and parallel branching; (ii) a recursive two-level execution design delegates each complex search sub-task to an inner Search Agent that runs its own planning loop, isolating noisy retrieval and stabilizing long-horizon execution; (iii) a rubric-based test-time optimization mechanism dynamically generates task-specific quality criteria and uses them as live reasoning scaffolds for evidence-grounded synthesis and adaptive stopping. Across two deep research benchmarks, DuMate-DeepResearch establishes new state-of-the-art results: the best overall score (58.03%) on DeepResearch Bench, and the best overall score (61.95%) on DeepResearch Bench II while ranking first in information recall and analysis.

</details>


### [94] [Off-Policy Evaluation with Strategic Agents via Local Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07308)
*Kiet Q. H. Vo,Abbavaram Gowtham Reddy,Julian Rodemann,Siu Lun Chau,Krikamol Muandet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究策略行为下的离线策略评估问题，提出通过事后解释披露局部信息来揭示智能体策略前的协变量，从而缓解策略行为导致的信息损失，并构建双重稳健估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法假设协变量外生于策略，但现实场景中智能体会策略性调整协变量以应对决策策略，导致协变量偏移。已有相关工作依赖强假设（如重复交互或完全了解智能体响应行为），限制了适用性。本文旨在仅需部分知识即可进行有效的离线策略评估。

Method: 采用一次性离线策略评估设定，决策者仅掌握智能体响应行为的部分知识。核心思路是通过事后解释披露局部信息，揭示智能体策略前的协变量。在此基础上估计智能体响应行为的统计模型，并构建双重稳健估计器用于策略值估计。假设智能体的成本敏感性服从条件对数正态分布。

Result: 证明了所提估计量的一致性，并通过实证实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 交互设计可以通过揭示智能体策略响应中原本隐藏的结构来缓解信息不对称问题。

Abstract: We study off-policy evaluation (OPE) under strategic behavior where decision subjects (or agents) respond to a decision maker's policy by strategically modifying their covariates. Such behavior induces a policy-dependent covariate shift, breaking the standard assumption in existing methods that covariates are exogenous to the policy. Related work addresses this challenge by imposing strong assumptions such as repeated interactions or full knowledge of agents' response behavior, substantially limiting its applicability to OPE. In contrast, we consider a one-shot OPE setting where the decision maker has only partial knowledge of the agents' response behavior. Our key insight is that disclosing local information through post-hoc explanations reveals agents' pre-strategic covariates prior to adaptation, mitigating the information loss induced by strategic behavior. Leveraging this structure, we estimate a statistical model for the agents' responses and construct a doubly robust estimator for policy value. By assuming that the agents' cost sensitivity follows a conditional log-normal distribution, we establish consistency of the proposed estimator and validate our approach empirically. More broadly, our results highlight how interaction design can mitigate information asymmetry by revealing otherwise hidden structure in agents' strategic responses.

</details>


### [95] [Online Pandora's Box for Contextual LLM Cascading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07392)
*Alexandre Belloni,Yan Chen,Yehua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线上下文Pandora's Box模型，用于自适应查询和选择LLM API。该模型采用两阶段决策：查询阶段（顺序查询API并产生输出相关成本）和选择阶段（选择输出部署并观察下游奖励）。与经典模型不同，奖励反馈是输出中介的。研究者直接对保留指数进行建模，结合GMM估计和UCB置信区间，实现了维度相关的$\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大语言模型（LLM）级联的启发，旨在解决如何自适应地查询和选择LLM API的问题，在未知输出分布和成本的情况下做出最优决策。

Method: 提出在线上下文Pandora's Box模型；对由Weitzman策略诱导的上下文保留指数函数施加参数化结构；采用GMM型估计结合UCB风格置信区间进行学习。

Result: 在正则条件下，证明了所提出的策略在T个时段上达到了维度相关的$\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$累积遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM API的在线自适应选择提供了理论保证的学习框架，有效处理了输出中介反馈这一非标准结构。

Abstract: Motivated by Large Language Model (LLM) cascading, we propose an online contextual Pandora's Box model for adaptively querying and selecting LLM APIs. In each period, a decision-maker observes a request context and faces a two-phase decision problem. In the query phase, the decision-maker sequentially queries APIs, where each query reveals a generated output and the decision-maker incurs an (output-dependent) cost. In the selection phase, the decision-maker selects one of the generated outputs to deploy and observes only the downstream reward of the deployed output. This output-mediated feedback structure differs from classical online contextual Pandora's Box models, in which opening a box directly reveals its reward.
  Rather than estimating the full conditional output and cost distributions of each API, we directly model the reservation index and develop a learning approach for the query phase. Specifically, we impose a parametric structure on the contextual reservation index functions induced by the classical Weitzman's policy. Our policy combines generalized method of moments (GMM) type estimation of these reservation indices with UCB-style confidence bounds for both these indices and the shared output-level reward evaluator. Under regularity conditions, we prove that the resulting policy achieves dimension-dependent $\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$ cumulative regret over a horizon of $T$ periods.

</details>


### [96] [Act As a Real Researcher: A Suite of Benchmarks Evaluating Frontier LLMs and Agentic Harnesses in Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07462)
*Jiayu Wang,Weijiang Lv,Bowen Fu,Jing Fu,Jiayi Song,Lingyu Zhang,Lanxuan Xue,Luodi Chen,Zepeng Xin,Kaiyu Li,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AARR系列基准测试旨在评估AI代理能否模拟人类研究者的专业素养与细微推理能力，首个基准AARRI-Bench测试显示最佳配置（Mini-SWE-Agent + Claude Opus 4.7）仅达到68.3%成功率，揭示了当前代理在细节敏感度上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估宏观执行能力，但前沿AI代理在领域敏感性、研究伦理和科学判断等细微层面存在显著局限，无法完全替代人类研究者，亟需针对这些能力缺口进行专门的基准测试。

Method: 提出AARR基准系列，其中AARRI-Bench关注细微研究场景中代理能否模拟人类研究者的专业性、彻底性和细致推理；在多个前沿模型和代理系统上进行了实验评估。

Result: 最佳配置（Mini-SWE-Agent + Claude Opus 4.7）成功率仅68.3%，代理经常忽略对人类研究者而言显而易见的细微但关键细节。

Conclusion: 开发类似人类研究者的AI需要进一步探索研究行为本身，而非仅仅构建复杂的外层框架。

Abstract: As foundation models advance and agent scaffolding becomes increasingly sophisticated, agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in complex, long-horizon coding tasks and even autonomous experiment execution. Despite their evolution from research assistants into autonomous research agents, these systems still exhibit significant limitations in field sensitivity, research ethics, and nuanced scientific judgment. Consequently, frontier agents remain unable to fully replace human researchers. To bridge this gap, we conceptualize the AARR (Act As a Real Researcher) benchmark series. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily assess macro-level execution capabilities, AARR focuses on whether agents can emulate the professionalism, thoroughness, and nuanced reasoning that characterize human researchers in granular research scenarios. In this work, we propose AARRI-Bench (Act As a Real Research Intern), the first benchmark in this series. We conduct extensive experiments across frontier models and agentic systems, revealing that even the best-performing configuration (Mini-SWE-Agent with Claude Opus 4.7) achieves only 68.3\% success rate, frequently overlooking subtle yet critical details that are obvious to real human researchers. Our results indicate that developing researcher-like AI requires further exploration of research behavior, rather than merely complex scaffolding. Our data is released at https://github.com/AARR-bench/AARRI-bench.

</details>


### [97] [How AI Agents Reshape Knowledge Work: Autonomy, Efficiency, and Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07489)
*Jeremy Yang,Kate Zyskowski,Noah Yonack,Jerry Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用Perplexity的Search和Computer产品生产数据，实证研究了AI智能体如何加速和重塑知识工作。研究发现：Computer每会话自主工作26分钟（Search仅33秒）， dissatisfaction率低55%；在匹配任务上将完成时间从269分钟降至36分钟，时间和成本分别降低87%和94%；Computer还扩展了用户工作范围，促使更多跨职业边界、高认知需求、复合型任务的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统正从对话助手向端到端自主执行任务的智能体演进，但这一转变如何实际影响知识工作的效率、质量和范围尚缺乏系统性实证证据。本文旨在通过真实生产数据量化AI智能体的这一影响。

Method: 使用Perplexity的Search（传统搜索产品）和Computer（自主智能体产品）的生产数据，将近似的初始查询对作为自然实验，比较同一任务在两种产品下的执行差异。分析会话时长、用户满意度、任务完成时间、查询语义分布等指标。

Result: 1) Computer每会话自主工作26分钟，Search仅33秒，Computer dissatisfaction率低55%；2) 匹配任务上完成时间从269分钟降至36分钟，时间和成本分别降低87%和94%；3) Computer查询更跨职业边界、需高阶认知、涉及更广专业知识、呈现复合任务特征，并解锁了Search中基本不出现的工作活动。

Conclusion: AI智能体不仅加速了工作流程、提升了产出质量、降低了成本，还显著扩展了自动化工作的广度和深度，改变了用户尝试完成的工作范围。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems are bridging the gap between intelligence and utility by shifting from conversational assistants to autonomous agents that execute tasks end to end. Using production data from Perplexity's Search and Computer products, we study this transition by examining how AI agents accelerate and reshape knowledge work. Three key empirical findings emerge. First, using sessions with near-identical initial query pairs as natural experiments for the same underlying task attempted with both products, Computer performs 26 minutes of autonomous work per user session, versus 33 seconds for Search. Computer automates task decomposition and execution that Search users might otherwise manually orchestrate and implement. As a result, Computer shifts follow-up query distribution toward higher-order work such as verification and extension. Autonomy also increases execution quality, with per-query dissatisfaction rates 55% lower on Computer than on Search. Second, due to its autonomy advantage, Computer reduces completion time from 269 to 36 minutes on matched tasks, lowering estimated time and cost by 87% and 94%, respectively, compared to humans equipped with Search alone. Third, Computer changes the scope of work that users attempt: Computer queries more often cross occupational boundaries, require higher-order cognition, draw on broader expertise, take the form of composite tasks that bundle interdependent subtasks into a single query, and unlock work activities that are essentially absent from Search usage among the same users. Together, the evidence indicates that AI agents accelerate workflows, enhance output quality, reduce costs, and expand the breadth and depth of automated work.

</details>


### [98] [Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510)
*Ahmed Alansary,Molham Mohamed,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种严重性感知的多模型框架，结合课程训练策略与相关性响应选择，用于医疗文本生成。该框架采用三阶段课程学习（轻、中、重症）训练五个大语言模型，在MAQA数据集上通过BERTScore评估，达到86.71%（基线）和90.30%（微调后）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗问答中难以根据病例严重程度提供一致且上下文合适的响应，缺乏对渐进式复杂医疗查询的适应能力。

Method: 提出严重性感知多模型框架：1）三阶段课程学习策略（轻、中、重症依次训练）；2）五个独立按相同课程方案训练的大语言模型；3）推理时所有模型生成候选响应，选择最合适的作为最终输出。

Result: 在MAQA数据集上，BERTScore评估：基线设置86.71%，微调后90.30%，优于基线和微调模型。

Conclusion: 课程学习与多模型响应选择相结合能有效提升医疗文本生成的质量和相关性。

Abstract: Telehealth systems have become increasingly important for delivering accessible and timely medical information. Existing large language models often struggle to provide consistent and contextually appropriate medical responses across varying levels of case severity. This limitation highlights the need for models that can effectively adapt to the progressive complexity in medical queries. To address this challenge, we introduce a severity-aware multi-model framework that integrates curriculum training strategy with relevance-based response selection. The proposed framework employs a three-stage curriculum learning strategy, where each model is trained sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical cases to progressively acquire domain knowledge. The approach utilizes five large language models, each independently trained under the same curriculum scheme. During inference, all models generate candidate responses, and the most appropriate response is selected as the final output. The framework is trained and evaluated on the MAQA dataset, which provides annotated medical question-answer pairs. Experimental results evaluated using BERTScore demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance compared to both baseline and fine-tuned models, attaining 86.71% in the baseline setting and 90.30% after fine-tuning. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining curriculum learning with multi-model response selection in improving response quality and relevance in medical text generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Enhancing Malware Detection with Generative AI: Using Variational Autoencoders to Boost Machine Learning Classifiers' Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06501)
*Mohammad Alharbi,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成合成恶意软件样本，结合随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等机器学习分类器，解决恶意软件数据稀缺问题。实验表明，经合成数据增强后的数据集显著提升了分类器的准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数，证明了生成式AI在网络安全领域的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件不断演进对网络安全构成挑战，需要先进的检测技术；同时，新型或罕见恶意软件类型存在严重的数据稀缺问题，限制了传统检测方法的有效性。

Method: 使用VAE生成高质量、多样化的合成恶意软件样本以增强原始数据集；将增强后的数据集分别输入随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型三种机器学习分类器进行训练和检测。

Result: 与仅在原始数据上训练相比，使用合成数据增强后的数据集训练的分类器在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均有显著提升，对多种恶意软件类别的检测性能均得到增强。

Conclusion: VAE生成合成数据的方法能有效提升恶意软件检测系统的鲁棒性和适应性，展示了生成式AI在网络安全领域的实用价值，并为未来构建更具弹性的检测系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The advancement of malware poses obstacles for cybersecurity, necessitating the development of advanced detection techniques. This paper proposes an approach to enhance malware detection through the use of a generative artificial intelligence model. Specifically, variational autoencoders (VAEs) are used with the random forest, XGBoost and sequential model machine learning classifiers. Generated synthetic malware samples are used to address the critical issue of data scarcity for new or less common malware types. This approach can be used to augment datasets to improve classifier robustness.
  The proposed methodology uses VAEs to create high-quality diverse synthetic datasets that closely mimic real-world malware data. The effectiveness of these augmented datasets is evaluated by comparing the performance of the machine learning classifiers when they are trained with the original data and when they are trained with the synthetic data-augmented datasets. The results demonstrate a notable improvement in the accuracy, precision, recall and F1-scores of the classifiers, when they are trained using the augmented datasets. The enhanced performance for detecting various malware classes shows the potential of this approach to facilitate adaptation to evolving malware threats effectively. This work demonstrates the utility of generative AI for cybersecurity. It also provides a foundation for future research aimed at developing more resilient and adaptive malware detection systems.

</details>


### [100] [Subtle Injection for Ground-truth Inference of LLM Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06502)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SIGIL框架，通过在受保护文本中嵌入不可感知的canary序列，使训练在该数据上的LLM展现出可统计检测的行为特征，从而实现训练数据成员推理。在36,000次试验中总体AUC达0.892，即使面对100%改写仍保持AUC>0.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在未经授权的情况下大量使用网络爬取数据进行训练，内容所有者需要一种取证方法，能够证明自己的文档是否被包含在模型的训练集中。

Method: 提出SIGIL框架，定义五种canary策略（词汇稀有、词汇短语、句法、语义、代码模式），通过Neyman-Pearson假设检验框架构建成员推理评分（MIS），实现形式化的假阳性率控制，并基于经验文献校准模拟参数。

Result: 36,000次试验总体AUC=0.892，注入率从0.1%到10%时AUC从0.831升至0.947；检测率33%-78%；Code Pattern策略AUC最高（0.903），Syntactic最低（0.875）；四个因素（注入率、模型规模、canary策略、混合比例）均对MIS有显著独立影响（p<0.001）；100%改写下AUC仍达0.864。

Conclusion: SIGIL能够有效且鲁棒地检测LLM是否在受保护数据上训练，即使在面对改写攻击时仍能通过语义泄露保持高检测性能，为版权所有者提供了一种可操作的取证工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained on scraped web corpora without authorisation, content owners require forensic methods to prove that their documents were included in a model's training set. We propose \textbf{SIGIL} (\textbf{S}ubtle \textbf{I}njection for \textbf{G}round-truth \textbf{I}nference of \textbf{L}LM training data), a framework that embeds imperceptible \emph{canary sequences} into protected text and code such that any LLM trained on those documents exhibits statistically detectable behavioural signatures when probed with targeted queries.
  SIGIL defines five canary strategies -- lexical-rare, lexical-phrase, syntactic, semantic, and code-pattern -- and a \emph{Membership Inference Score} (MIS) grounded in the Neyman-Pearson hypothesis testing framework with formal false-positive rate (FPR) control. Simulator parameters are calibrated against the empirical membership inference literature, yielding realistic heterogeneous results across $36{,}000$ trials: overall AUC $= 0.892$, rising from $0.831$ at $0.1\%$ injection to $0.947$ at $10\%$. Detection rates range from $33\%$ to $78\%$ across model-size and injection-rate conditions. Code Pattern canaries achieve the highest AUC ($0.903$, Cohen's $d = 1.84$); Syntactic Structure the lowest ($0.875$, $d = 1.63$). All four experimental factors -- injection rate, model size, canary strategy, and mixture ratio -- have significant independent effects on MIS ($p < 0.001$). SIGIL maintains AUC $> 0.86$ even under $100\%$ paraphrasing ($\text{AUC} = 0.864$), confirming robustness through semantic leakage that survives surface-level rewriting.

</details>


### [101] [MalTree: Tracing Malware Evolution from Embeddings at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06570)
*Akash Amalan,Georgios Smaragdakis,Tom J. Viering*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MalTree框架，借鉴生物信息学的系统发育技术（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining），利用结构、行为和图像特征自动建模恶意软件演化关系，实现87%的时间一致性，并发现不同家族突变速率差异可达10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测是被动的，模型随着威胁演变而性能下降。逆向工程揭示恶意软件家族间的演化关系需要数月到数年，缺乏自动化的大规模演化分析方法。

Method: 采用生物信息学启发的系统发育技术（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining），使用结构、行为和图像特征自动建模恶意软件演化。引入基于VirusTotal时间戳的时间验证来评估推断树是否反映真实演化顺序。

Result: MalTree达到87%的时间一致性，推断的演化关系与真实出现时间线高度吻合。发现不同家族突变速率差异可达10倍以上。Mirai僵尸网络案例研究证实推断关系与已知威胁情报一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为恶意软件分析从逐样本分类转向谱系感知的演化建模奠定了基础，建议根据家族特异性进化节奏定制检测策略。

Abstract: Malware detection remains largely reactive: machine learning models trained on known samples degrade as threats evolve. Understanding evolutionary relationships among malware families can inform proactive defense, but traditional reverse engineering can take months to years to uncover such lineage relationships. We propose MalTree, a framework that applies bioinformatics inspired phylogenetic techniques (UPGMA and Neighbor-Joining) at scale to model malware evolution automatically using structural, behavioral, and image-based features. We introduce temporal validation using VirusTotal timestamps to assess whether inferred trees reflect actual evolutionary order. MalTree achieves 87% temporal consistency, indicating that inferred evolutionary relationships closely align with real-world emergence timelines. Our analysis shows that some families mutate over 10 times faster than others, suggesting that detection strategies should be tailored to family-specific evolutionary tempos. Case studies, including the Mirai botnet, confirm that inferred relationships from our phylogenetic tree align with documented threat intelligence. Our framework provides a foundation for shifting malware analysis from sample-by-sample classification toward lineage-aware evolutionary modeling.

</details>


### [102] [AgileOS: A GPU Operating System Layer for Protected CUDA Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06697)
*Zhuoping Yang,Yiyu Shi,Alex Jones,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgileOS是一个GPU操作系统层，通过库边界虚拟化CUDA、分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO范围的GPU内存管理模型、以及PTX级别的内核内存防护，为受保护的CUDA服务提供操作系统级别的隔离保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU应用越来越多地与存储系统、网络设备、厂商库和GPU常驻服务交互，而非仅执行隔离的计算内核。这种转变需要对GPU服务提供操作系统级别的保护，但当前的CUDA编程模型默认将CUDA上下文、设备指针、运行时句柄、模块加载路径和内核启动等直接暴露给不受信任的应用内核，导致受保护的GPU服务只能构建临时性的接口和隔离机制。

Method: AgileOS在库边界虚拟化CUDA：应用程序链接客户端CUDA Runtime、Driver和选定库的shim层，而可信的运行时工作者拥有真正的CUDA上下文并中介支持的操作。定义了GPU内存管理模型，通过指针验证和PTX注入的内存访问守卫，将用户分配与受保护的模块/MMIO范围分离。原型包括客户端拦截器、工作者端CUDA处理器、虚拟化CUDA对象表、受保护AgileOS模块、GPU内存管理器、选定的可信库适配器以及PTX级内核内存守卫。

Result: 原型实现了客户端拦截器、工作者端CUDA处理器、虚拟化CUDA对象表、受保护AgileOS模块、分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO范围的GPU内存管理器、选定的可信库适配器以及PTX级内核内存守卫。

Conclusion: AgileOS通过模块化和灵活的设计，支持一系列受保护服务和现有库（如cuFFT和PyTorch），为GPU服务提供了操作系统级别的隔离保护机制。

Abstract: Modern GPU applications increasingly interact with storage systems, network devices, vendor libraries, and GPU-resident services rather than executing only isolated compute kernels. This shift creates a need for operating-system-like protection around GPU services, where service metadata, device queues, memory-mapped I/O regions, and library-internal state should not be directly exposed to untrusted application kernels. However, today's CUDA programming model, by default, still gives each application direct ownership of its CUDA context, device pointers, runtime handles, module loading path, and kernel launches, leaving protected GPU services to build their own ad hoc interfaces and isolation mechanisms.
  This paper presents the initial design and prototype scope of AgileOS, a GPU operating-system layer for protected CUDA services. AgileOS virtualizes CUDA at the library boundary: applications link against client-side CUDA Runtime, Driver, and selected library shims, while a trusted runtime worker owns the real CUDA context and mediates supported operations. To protect service state and module interfaces, AgileOS also defines a GPU memory-management model that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, using pointer validation and memory access guards via PTX injection. AgileOS is modularized and flexible, supporting a range of protected services and existing libraries such as cuFFT and PyTorch. The prototype includes client-side interceptors, worker-side CUDA handlers, virtualized CUDA object tables, protected AgileOS modules, a GPU memory manager that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, selected trusted library adapters, and the PTX-level kernel memory guard.

</details>


### [103] [The Custody Envelope Threshold: Authority-Scaled Admission of External Artifacts in Institutional Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06767)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出**托管信封阈值（Custody Envelope Threshold）**，一个基于权限规模的工件准入模型，用于评估机构对外部维护工件的防御性准入决策，并通过四条件序数量表进行操作化，应用于多种现代基础设施工件类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础设施高度依赖外部维护的工件（如包依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像、Terraform提供者、AI模型工件等），这些工件易于获取但难以被机构治理、准入和撤销，存在安全风险与治理缺口。

Method: 提出**托管信封阈值**框架，以对象身份、入口路径、撤销能力的封闭性相对于工件所授予的执行权限是否足够为依据，判断直接机构准入是否可辩护；当阈值不满足时，机构倾向于代理、策略调解、供应商调解、内部化、隔离或拒绝。框架通过**四条件序数量表**操作化，并与引用监视器推理、最小权限原则、交易成本经济学关联。

Result: 框架应用于包依赖、GitHub Actions、容器镜像、Terraform提供者/模块、开发者扩展及开放模型工件；Model Context Protocol (MCP) 服务器作为留出证据。论文还指定了验证设计、确定性预测函数和OSF复制包，用于检验高审查机构是否对高权限工件趋向更强的托管封闭性。

Conclusion: 当机构对被授权工件无法实现足够的身份、路径和撤销封闭性时，直接准入不可辩护，应转向代理或内部化等替代策略；该框架为工件治理提供了理论驱动的可操作评估工具。

Abstract: Modern infrastructure depends on externally maintained artifacts such as package-registry dependencies, CI/CD actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, model artifacts, and AI tool servers. These artifacts are easy to fetch but difficult for institutions to admit, govern, and revoke. This paper proposes the Custody Envelope Threshold, an authority-scaled model of artifact admission. It argues that direct institutional admission is defensible only when object identity, ingress path, and revocation capacity are sufficiently closed relative to the execution authority delegated to the artifact. When this threshold is not met, institutions tend to proxy, policy-mediate, vendor-mediate, internalize, quarantine, or reject the artifact. The framework is operationalized as a four-condition ordinal instrument and connected to reference-monitor reasoning, least privilege, and transaction cost economics. It is applied to package dependencies, GitHub Actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, and open model artifacts, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers treated as held-out evidence. The paper also specifies a validation design, deterministic prediction function, and OSF replication package for testing whether high-scrutiny institutions converge toward stronger custody closure for high-authority artifacts.

</details>


### [104] [What Your Posts Reveal: A Benchmark and Agentic Framework for User-Level Privacy Leakage on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06784)
*Zifan Peng,Yini Huang,Aiwen Lu,Qiming Ye,Peixian Zhang,Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Xuechao Wang,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了SopriBench基准和隐私暴露评分(PES)，以及基于溯因推理的Argus框架，在跨帖子隐私泄露推理上相比最强基线提升25% PES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一的用户级多模态隐私泄露基准，以及能够刻画暴露严重程度（而非仅二元准确率）的评估指标。

Method: 1) 基于Rednote和Instagram私有关联语料库抽象出泄露模式，构建包含50个用户档案和1,569张图像的SopriBench基准；2) 提出PES指标，按上下文敏感度加权值粒度；3) 提出Argus框架，通过溯因推理形成假设、验证支持证据、聚合跨帖子线索形成隐私画像。

Result: Argus达到0.55 PES，比最强基线提升25%，在跨帖子泄露场景上增益最大。

Conclusion: 所提出的基准、评估指标和推理框架能有效捕捉多模态跨帖子隐私泄露，且无需训练即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Public social media posts can reveal private information through weak cues scattered across text, images, or metadata. Such leakage is often cumulative and cross-post: cues that appear harmless in isolation may jointly expose a user's home, workplace, or routine. However, current research lacks a unified benchmark for user-level multimodal privacy leakage and an evaluation metric that captures exposure severity beyond binary accuracy.
  To address these gaps, we propose SopriBench, a synthetic benchmark guided by leakage patterns abstracted from a private reference corpus of Rednote and Instagram accounts, covering 50 user profiles and 1,569 images with attributes, contextual sensitivity, granularity, leakage type, inference difficulty, and supporting evidence. We further introduce the Privacy Exposure Score (PES), which weights value granularity by contextual sensitivity. Inspired by abductive reasoning, we introduce Argus, a training-free agentic framework for cumulative leakage inference. Argus forms hypotheses from accumulated evidence, verifies supporting evidence, and aggregates cross-post cues into privacy profiles, achieving 0.55 PES, a 25% improvement over the strongest baseline, with the largest gain on cross-post leakage.

</details>


### [105] [AMD-FCG: An Enhanced Function Call Graph Dataset with Integrated Topological Features for Malware Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06815)
*Parthajit Borah,Sakshi Singh,D. K. Bhattacharyya,J. K. Kalita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMD-FCG，一个增强的函数调用图数据集，通过整合恶意软件的拓扑特征来提升恶意软件检测的效率和准确性，无需动态分析和大量处理即可用于开发创新的检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件结构复杂、行为多样，传统检测方法难以有效应对，需要一种可靠且自适应的解决方案来覆盖多种恶意软件家族。

Method: 利用函数调用图（FCG）提取恶意软件的结构和行为模式，构建增强数据集AMD-FCG，整合拓扑特征，消除动态分析需求。

Result: AMD-FCG数据集包含多样化的恶意软件和良性应用样本，能够有效覆盖不同恶意软件家族，为检测系统提供准确性和鲁棒性基础。

Conclusion: AMD-FCG通过简化检测流程，使网络安全专业人员能够更高效地开发和部署创新性恶意软件检测系统，是一种实用且高效的数据集方案。

Abstract: As malware illustrates a complex structure and behavior, detection of these has been a significant challenge in the domain of cybersecurity along with related services in daily life. So, it becomes crucial to have a reliable and adaptive solution to address the issue. Among the several detection methods developed over the years, one of the most reliable ones is studying and analyzing the structural and behavioral patterns of malware. These patterns of sophisticated malware can be obtained with the help of Function Call Graphs (FCGs). However, to effectively cover numerous groups of families of malware, it is required to have a sufficiently large dataset for the system to operate on. In order to ensure accuracy and robustness of the system, the dataset should comprise samples of different malwares and a benign application for secure execution of the detection process. This paper introduces AMD-FCG, an enhanced Function Call Graph dataset integrated with topological features of malwares. The framework enhances the detection procedure, streamlining the workflow for cybersecurity professionals and also eliminating the need for dynamic analysis and extensive processing. Therefore, it can be used to develop and deploy more efficient and innovative malware detection systems.

</details>


### [106] [On the Incentive Compatibility of Block Propagation in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06860)
*Fumichika Maeda,Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币矿工在区块传播中的激励问题，发现矿工没有动力转发其他矿工产生的区块，并在不同分叉解决规则下分析了传播延迟、算力分布与挖矿公平性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的去中心化特性依赖于矿工自愿传播区块，但矿工作为理性经济个体可能存在不传播区块的激励，这将是比特币激励机制的根本缺陷。此前研究未充分刻画不同分叉解决规则下矿工改善区块传播的激励。

Method: 建立区块链网络模型，推导不同分叉解决规则下的挖矿收益解析表达式，显式刻画区块传播延迟、算力分布和分叉解决规则如何共同决定挖矿收益，并基于此分析矿工的传播激励。

Result: 矿工没有挖矿收益上的激励去转发其他矿工产生的区块；在先到先得规则下，每个非多数派矿工都有激励更快接收和传播自己的区块；不同规则在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间存在权衡，先到先得规则传播激励最强但公平性最差。

Conclusion: 比特币的区块传播激励机制存在缺陷，矿工缺乏转发他人区块的动力；传播激励与挖矿公平性不可兼得，需根据系统目标权衡选择分叉解决规则。

Abstract: Bitcoin is permissionless and does not rely on any central administrator, which gives it strong censorship resistance. At the same time, it is important to incentivize miners to behave in ways that align with the interests of the system as a whole. This paper asks whether miners are individually incentivized to propagate blocks, one of the most fundamental processes in Bitcoin. Miners collectively maintain the blockchain by generating blocks and disseminating them across the network. If miners have an incentive not to propagate some blocks, this would indicate a fundamental flaw in Bitcoin's incentive design. Although prior work has studied how propagation delays affect forks and mining rewards, it has not fully characterized miners' incentives to improve block propagation under different tie-breaking rules. To address this gap, we derive analytical reward expressions for each tie-breaking rule based on a blockchain network model that captures the effect of forks on mining fairness. These expressions explicitly characterize how block propagation delays, hashrate distribution, and tie-breaking rules jointly determine mining rewards. We then use them to analyze miners' incentives to improve block propagation. Our results show, for example, that miners have no mining-reward incentive to relay blocks generated by other miners. By contrast, under the first-seen rule, every non-majority miner is incentivized to receive other miners' blocks more quickly and to propagate its own blocks more quickly. Finally, we compare tie-breaking rules and identify a trade-off between propagation incentives and mining fairness. In particular, the first-seen rule provides the strongest incentives to reduce propagation delays, but it also worsens mining fairness the most.

</details>


### [107] [FDM: A Framework for Decision-making to build ML-based Malware detection systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06894)
*Tadiwa Vhito,Jakapan Suaboot,Warodom Werapun,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出决策框架(FDM)，通过加权配置兼容性得分(WCCS)对五个操作参数进行多准则评分，为九种配置维度提供排序推荐，以解决恶意软件检测中ML配置选择的复杂多目标问题。实验验证了最优ML配置具有上下文依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择合适的机器学习配置用于恶意软件检测是一个复杂的多准则问题，模型选择、特征工程和更新机制必须联合满足不同部署场景下的操作约束，现有方法缺乏系统化的决策框架。

Method: 提出FDM框架，使用WCCS多准则评分函数，映射五个操作参数（平台约束、资源预算、响应延迟、更新频率、检测灵敏度）至九个配置维度的排序推荐。在三个数据集（私有Windows API、Malimg图像基准、Android静态API）上进行四项实验验证。

Result: (i) XGBoost在二分类中达到最佳精度-资源比（97.46%测试精度，<70MB RAM），优于LSTM/BiLSTM（高达2.8GB）；(ii) 多分类中经典模型（XGBoost 79.03%）优于循环深度模型（BiLSTM 72.27%），二分类排名反转；(iii) 类增量学习EfficientNetB0在11步中保持99.13%精度，仅下降0.65pp；(iv) 迁移学习平均减少2.14倍训练时间；(v) 自编码器预处理实现14倍训练加速，仅损失0.86pp精度。

Conclusion: 最优ML配置具有上下文依赖性，验证了FDM框架的核心前提及其对网络安全从业者的实用价值。

Abstract: Selecting appropriate machine learning (ML) configurations for malware detection is a complex, multi-criteria problem. Model choice, feature engineering, and update mechanisms must jointly satisfy operational constraints that vary across deployment contexts. This paper proposes the Framework for Decision-making (FDM) to build ML-based malware detection systems. The FDM formalises this selection process using the Weighted Configuration Compatibility Score (WCCS), a multi-criteria scoring function mapping five operational parameters (platform constraint, resource budget, response latency, update frequency, and detection sensitivity) to ranked recommendations across nine configuration dimensions. To validate the framework, four experiments were conducted on three datasets (a private Windows API dataset, the public Malimg image benchmark, and an Android static API dataset). Key results include: (i) XGBoost achieved the best accuracy-to-resource ratio in binary classification (97.46 % test accuracy, <70 MB RAM), outperforming LSTM/BiLSTM which consumed up to 2.8 GB; (ii) in multi-class classification, classical models (XGBoost 79.03 %) outperformed recurrent deep models (BiLSTM 72.27 %), reversing the binary ranking; (iii) class-incremental learning with EfficientNetB0 maintained 99.13 % accuracy with only 0.65 pp degradation across 11 incremental steps; (iv) transfer learning reduced training time by 2.14 times on average for image-based malware data without significant accuracy cost; and (v) autoencoder pre-processing yielded a 14 times training speedup at a cost of only 0.86 pp accuracy. These findings confirm that the optimal ML configuration is context-dependent, validating the FDM's core premise and demonstrating its practical utility for cybersecurity practitioners.

</details>


### [108] [Blockchain Infrastructure for Intelligent Cyber--Physical--Social Systems:Post-Quantum Security, Interoperability, and Trustworthy Data Economies in the Era of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06895)
*Song Guo,Huawei Huang,Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本教程探讨了将区块链作为协调层，在体现场景智能与量子计算威胁的双重转型中，构建抗量子、可互操作、数据可信的去中心化智能基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体现场景智能（世界模型机器人）对区块链提出数据溯源、跨组织治理与激励兼容共享的需求；同时量子计算发展威胁现有密码学体系。两者共同催生了对长期可验证、抗量子攻击的加密敏捷架构的迫切需求。

Method: 通过五个集成模块展开：体现场景智能与世界模型需求、量子硬件现实与证据驱动的安全迁移、基于BrokerChain的可扩展跨分片架构、Croissant元数据标准与机器人学习溯源的可信数据经济、多模态云部署的产业生态集成，并辅以AWS Braket量子硬件演示。

Result: 展示了从ECDSA到后量子签名的迁移过程，提出了BrokerChain跨分片协议，实现了Croissant元数据标准与机器人学习溯源的结合，提供了开源框架与路线图。

Conclusion: 区块链可作为统一基础设施，桥接量子硬件现实与体现场景智能数据需求，构建下一代抗量子、可互操作、数据可信的去中心化智能环境。

Abstract: The deployment of embodied artificial intelligence via world-model-based robotics presents a transformative opportunity for blockchain infrastructure, establishing urgent demand for trustworthy data provenance, cross-organizational governance, and incentive-compatible sharing across decentralized ecosystems. Simultaneously, quantum computing advances recognized by the 2025 Nobel Prize in Physics and the Turing Award threaten the cryptographic primitives securing these data economies, creating an interdependent imperative: long-lived verification for embodied AI depends on crypto-agile architectures capable of withstanding quantum adversaries. This tutorial examines blockchain as the coordination layer bridging this dual transition, from financial substrate to foundational Cyber-Physical-Social Systems infrastructure that simultaneously secures against quantum cryptanalysis and enables scalable, trustworthy data economies. The session opens with an immersive AWS Braket demonstration engaging participants with superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom hardware to assess cryptographic threat timelines and witness ECDSA-to-post-quantum signature transitions. Five integrated modules progress from embodied AI and world-model requirements through quantum hardware reality and evidence-based security migration, to scalable cross-shard architectures via BrokerChain protocols, trustworthy data economies implementing Croissant metadata standards and robotic learning provenance, and industry ecosystem integration for multi-modal cloud deployment. By bridging quantum hardware realities with embodied AI data requirements, this tutorial charts blockchain as unified infrastructure for next-generation decentralized intelligent environments, providing open-source frameworks and roadmaps for architecting quantum-resistant, interoperable, and data-trustworthy systems.

</details>


### [109] [DPAgent-in-the-Middle: Agentic Defense and Repair Against AI-Groomed Deceptive Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06914)
*Zewei Shi,Ruoxi Sun,Haoyang Li,Seong Oun Hwang,Feng Liu,Minhui Xue,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Grooming威胁模型，并设计DPAgent框架——一种基于多智能体协作的主动防御系统，通过潜在空间净化与防御性提示相结合，在真实网页环境中主动探测、检测并修复隐私欺骗性模式。实验表明DPAgent在检测率（90.98%）、检测F1（0.816）、探索效率（仅需基线10%的页面）和修复成功率（77%）上均达到最优效果，大规模网站研究发现高达98%的网站存在隐私欺骗模式，其中90%以上可被DPAgent缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对网页界面中隐私欺骗模式的防御手段存在碎片化、静态化的问题，且日益受到大语言模型操纵的威胁。同时，网络生态中的"数据空洞"（data voids）为攻击者注入误导性内容提供了温床，这些内容可被AI系统抓取学习，从而放大欺骗性设计和模型不良行为。亟需一种能够主动应对这一新型威胁的防御框架。

Method: 形式化定义AI Grooming威胁模型；提出DPAgent框架，协调四个专门化智能体（探索、检测、修复、防御），通过潜在空间净化（latent space purification）与防御性提示（defensive prompting）相结合的方式，在真实网页环境中主动探测、检测并修复隐私欺骗性用户界面，采用"中间智能体"（agent-in-the-middle）的主动防御范式。

Result: DPAgent检测到90.98%的groomed样本；隐私欺骗模式检测达到SOTA水平，微F1为0.816；仅访问基线约10%的页面即可探索超过80%的模式类型；成功修复77%的被检测欺骗界面；对485个网站的野外观测发现高达98%包含至少一个隐私欺骗模式，其中90%以上可被缓解；用户研究证实DPAgent有效降低隐私风险且不影响浏览体验。

Conclusion: DPAgent作为一种"中间智能体"防御范式，在保障网页UI供应链安全、抵御欺骗性设计以及基于数据空洞利用的新兴AI威胁方面展现出显著潜力。

Abstract: Privacy deceptive patterns in web interfaces systematically manipulate users into disclosing personal data, yet existing defenses are fragmented, static, and increasingly vulnerable to manipulation by large language models. Moreover, data voids, areas of information scarcity within the web ecosystem, create fertile ground for adversaries to inject misleading content that can be scraped and learned by AI systems, thereby amplifying both deceptive design and model misbehavior. In this paper, we formalize a new threat model, AI grooming, where attackers exploit data voids to seed benign-looking but malicious samples that corrupt model reasoning and normalize deceptive practices. To address this threat in privacy deceptive patterns, we present DPAgent, an agentic and reasoning-aware framework that orchestrates four specialized agents to mitigate the AI Grooming threat via a proactive defense that combines latent space purification with defensive prompting and operates directly in live web environments to proactively explore, detect, and repair privacy deceptive user interfaces before they reach end users. Extensive evaluations show that DPAgent detects 90.98% of groomed samples, achieves state-of-the-art privacy deceptive pattern detection with a micro F1 of 0.816, explores over 80% of pattern types while visiting only about 10% of the pages required by baselines, and successfully repairs 77% of detected deceptive interfaces. A large-scale study of 485 websites in the wild reveals that up to 98% contain at least one privacy deceptive pattern, over 90% of which can be mitigated by DPAgent. User studies further confirm that DPAgent effectively reduces privacy risks while preserving browsing experience. Our results demonstrate the promise of agent-in-the-middle defenses for securing the web UI supply chain against deceptive design and emerging AI threats rooted in data void exploitation.

</details>


### [110] [HAVE: Host Active Verification Engine for Closing the Contextual Reality Gap in Security Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06968)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Marco Pasquini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出主机主动验证引擎(HAVE)，通过部署安全约束的主机代理进行经验性贝努利试验，以最大似然估计测量实际妥协概率，并用威尔逊区间置信权重通过贝叶斯混合规则修正CVSS分数带来的上下文现实差距。评估显示HAVE可将误报场景的$P_{\text{reach}}$降低38.2%，漏报场景提升132.4%，净修正+124.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全数字孪生(SDT)依赖理论CVSS分数分配横向移动概率，导致上下文现实差距——未被承认的缓解措施使风险被高估，逻辑缺陷绕过内存安全防御时风险被严重低估。

Method: 提出HAVE扩展，部署安全约束主机代理，通过快照隔离的伯努利试验进行最大似然估计测量经验妥协概率$\hat{p}$；使用威尔逊区间宽度置信权重$\alpha_w$，通过贝叶斯混合规则（形式化关联Beta-Binomial后验）将$\hat{p}$传播到蒙特卡洛模拟中。

Result: 在四个漏洞类别、三个安全层级和两个生产二进制文件上的评估显示：HAVE在误报场景中降低$P_{\text{reach}}$ 38.2%，在漏报场景中提升132.4%，净修正+124.1%；后HAVE估计在不同校准指数$\kappa$下仅变化1.12倍，而CVSS基线变化4.6倍。

Conclusion: HAVE通过经验测量有效弥合了SDT中的上下文现实差距，显著提升了横向移动概率估计的准确性，且对校准参数具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Security Digital Twins (SDTs) provide continuously updated virtual replicas of infrastructure for threat simulation, yet they rely on theoretical CVSS scores to assign lateral-movement probabilities -- creating the Contextual Reality Gap: risk is overestimated where unacknowledged mitigations neutralize exploits, and drastically underestimated where logic flaws bypass all memory-safety defenses. We present the Host Active Verification Engine (HAVE), an SDT extension that deploys a safety-constrained host agent to measure the empirical probability of compromise $\hat{p}$ via maximum-likelihood estimation over snapshot-isolated Bernoulli trials. A Wilson interval-width confidence weight $α_w$ propagates $\hat{p}$ into Monte Carlo simulations via a Bayesian blending rule formally related to the Beta-Binomial posterior. Evaluation across four vulnerability classes, three security tiers, and two production binaries shows HAVE reduces $P_{\text{reach}}$ by 38.2% in false-positive scenarios and increases it by 132.4% in false-negative scenarios, with a net +124.1% correction; post-HAVE estimates vary by only $1.12\times$ across calibration exponents $κ$, versus $4.6\times$ for CVSS-only baselines.

</details>


### [111] [The Sound of Malware: A Memory Forensics Approach for Android Malware Analysis via Audio Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07005)
*Silvia Lucia Sanna,Massimo Palozzi,Leonardo Regano,Riccardo Lazzeretti,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内存取证和音频波形转换的安卓恶意软件检测框架，将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照直接映射为音频波形，利用频谱描述符、CNN和Transformer嵌入进行分类，在CICMalDroid2020和VirusTotal数据集上达到98.0%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态和动态特征（如权限、API调用、控制流）在面对代码混淆、动态加载、加壳及隐写操纵等高级逃逸策略时效果显著下降，基于签名和显式语义指标的机器学习模型可靠性降低，亟需一种不依赖语义特征工程的鲁棒检测方法。

Method: 将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照通过直接二进制到波形的映射转换为音频波形，保留底层结构模式；提取手工频谱描述符，并利用卷积神经网络和Transformer嵌入对信号进行处理和分类。

Result: 在CICMalDroid2020数据集和VirusTotal恶意软件上达到98.0%的准确率，优于静态声谱化方法和当前最先进的技术。

Conclusion: 基于内存取证和信号化结构表示的方法能够有效绕过代码混淆和隐写等逃逸技术，为安卓恶意软件检测提供了一种无需反汇编和特征工程的新范式，具有高准确率和强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Android malware analysis is currently facing increasing challenges in achieving robust classification and detecting stealth attacks. Modern threats employ advanced evasion strategies such as code obfuscation, dynamic loading, packing, and even steganographic manipulation of traditional static and dynamic features. These techniques reduce the effectiveness of signature-based systems and degrade the reliability of Machine Learning models that depend on explicit semantic indicators such as permissions, API calls, or control-flow structures. In this work, we propose \approachname, a memory forensics malware detection framework that shifts the analysis perspective from semantic program modeling to signal-based structural representation. Both static bytecode and early-execution memory snapshots are transformed into audio waveforms through direct binary-to-waveform mapping, preserving low-level structural patterns without requiring disassembly or feature engineering. The resulting signals are processed using handcrafted spectral descriptors, Convolutional Neural Networks, and transformer-based embeddings. Experiments on CICMalDroid2020 dataset and VirusTotal malware demonstrate that \approachname achieves up to 98.0\% accuracy, outperforming static sonification and competitive state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [112] [Fast Bounded-Independence Functions and Their Duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07009)
*Martijn Brehm,Yuval Ishai,Nicolas Resch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有随机函数有用属性的快速函数（线性规模电路可计算），在三个方面取得进展：构造了代数度最优的快速t-wise独立哈希函数；改进了快速码及其对偶码的构造，使其达到Gilbert-Varshamov界且具有可忽略失败概率；首次实现了将任意t个线性无关输入映射为均匀独立输出的快速线性函数族。这些结果在密码学中首次实现了电路复杂度与参与方数量呈线性关系的完美安全多方计算协议，以及最优渐近电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学应用需要兼具高效计算（线性规模电路）和随机函数优良性质的函数，现有结果在代数度、编码构造的失败概率、以及更强随机性质（如组合列表解码）方面存在不足。

Method: 通过代数度与电路大小的联合优化构造快速t-wise独立哈希函数；通过改进编码构造方法，支持一般域和码率、系统编码、快速通用编码器，并实现可忽略失败概率；构造将任意t个线性无关输入映射为均匀独立输出的快速线性函数族。

Result: 同时优化了快速t-wise独立哈希函数的电路规模和代数度；改进了快速码构造，使其达到GV界、失败概率可忽略、支持一般域和码率；首次实现了t>1时任意t个线性无关输入到均匀独立输出的快速线性映射；基于上述结果实现了首个电路复杂度与参与方数呈线性关系的完美安全多方计算协议和最优渐近复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积协议。

Conclusion: 论文在快速随机函数构造方面取得了系统性进展，填补了多个理论空白，并在密码学中展示了重要的应用价值，特别是在多方计算和加密计算领域实现了渐近最优的电路复杂度。

Abstract: We continue the study of {\em fast} functions, computable by linear-size circuits, that share useful properties of random functions. Motivated by cryptographic applications, we generalize and improve on previous results in this area, obtaining the following results:
  - For any constant $t$, we construct a fast $t$-wise independent hash function with algebraic degree $\log_2 t$ (over $\mathbb F_2$), simultaneously optimizing both asymptotic circuit size and degree.
  - We simplify and improve a recent construction (ITCS 2026) of a family of fast codes with fast duals, both meeting the Gilbert-Varshamov bound. Unlike the previous construction, our construction has negligible failure probability, can accommodate general fields and rates, supports a systematic encoding, and admits fast universal encoders.
  - We strengthen the above to support stronger random-like properties, such as optimal combinatorial list-decoding. This is achieved by constructing, for any constant $t$, a family of fast linear functions that map any $t$ linearly independent inputs to uniform and statistically independent outputs. Prior to our work, this was only known for $t=1$.
  We demonstrate the usefulness of the above results to cryptography. This includes the first nontrivial protocols for perfectly secure multiparty computation whose circuit complexity scales linearly with the number of parties, as well as protocols for computing encrypted matrix-vector products with optimal asymptotic circuit complexity.

</details>


### [113] [MalSkillBench: A Runtime-Verified Benchmark of Malicious Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07131)
*Wenbo Guo,Wei Zeng,Chengwei Liu,Xiaojun Jia,Yijia Xu,Lei Tang,Yong Fang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了MalSkillBench——首个经过运行时验证的恶意AI代理技能基准测试，包含3,944个恶意技能（按108类三维分类法标注），通过闭环生成-验证-反馈流水线构建。实验发现：代码注入验证成功率94.5%而提示注入仅75.8%；最强技能检测器在代码注入上召回率达98.4%，但在提示注入和代理控制攻击上表现崩溃；仅用野外样本评估会使排名波动高达66个召回点。供应链扫描器和提示注入防御各只能看到技能的一半，检测恶意技能需要联合推理任务意图、代码和指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理（如Claude Code、Gemini CLI）越来越多地通过第三方技能扩展自身，这些技能同时包含自然语言指令、可执行脚本和工具权限，形成了既非纯代码也非纯提示的混合供应链依赖风险。现有检测工具从未针对经过验证的这一混合空间地面真相进行评测，其有效性未知且仅基于野外数据的评估存在偏差。

Method: 构建了闭环Generate-Verify-Feedback流水线：在Docker沙箱中运行技能，通过系统调用监控和LLM裁判验证恶意行为是否实际触发。生成了3,214个经运行时验证的恶意技能，加上703个野外样本和4,000个匹配的良性技能，按108类三维分类法标注（涵盖代码注入、提示注入、代理控制攻击等维度）。

Result: 代码注入验证成功率94.5%，提示注入仅75.8%；野外样本高度集中（86.6%为单一行为，81%来自两个账号）；最强技能检测器在代码注入上召回率98.4%，但在提示注入和代理控制攻击上崩溃；纯野外评估导致排名波动达66个召回点；供应链扫描器和提示注入防御各自仅检测技能的一半特征，组合也无法恢复代码-指令关系。

Conclusion: 检测恶意技能需要联合推理任务意图、代码和指令三个维度，现有单一检测手段均存在严重盲区。发布了数据集、流水线、基线和结果以推动该领域研究。
